</div></div></div><div class="opblock-section"><div class="opblock-section-header"><div class="tab-header"><h4 class="opblock-title">Parameters</h4></div><div class="try-out"><button class="btn try-out__btn">Try it out </button></div></div><div class="parameters-container"><div class="table-container"><table class="parameters"><thead><tr><th class="col_header parameters-col_name">Name</th><th class="col_header parameters-col_description">Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr data-param-name="authorization" data-param-in="header"><td class="parameters-col_name"><div class="parameter__name">authorization</div><div class="parameter__type">string</div><div class="parameter__deprecated"></div><div class="parameter__in">(header)</div></td><td class="parameters-col_description"><div class="markdown"><p>The user's API token preceded by the keyword <code>Bearer</code> (with space between it and the token). </div><input type="text" class="" title="" placeholder="authorization" disabled="" value=""></td></tr><tr data-param-name="x-tenant-id" data-param-in="header"><td class="parameters-col_name"><div class="parameter__name">x-tenant-id</div><div class="parameter__type">string</div><div class="parameter__deprecated"></div><div class="parameter__in">(header)</div></td><td class="parameters-col_description"><div class="markdown"><p>Used to define the Tenant ID on behalf of whom the operation will be executed</p>
</div><input type="text" class="" title="" placeholder="x-tenant-id" disabled="" value=""></td></tr><tr data-param-name="fields" data-param-in="query"><td class="parameters-col_name"><div class="parameter__name">fields</div><div class="parameter__type">string</div><div class="parameter__deprecated"></div><div class="parameter__in">(query)</div></td><td class="parameters-col_description"><div class="markdown"><p>Whitelist of fields to return for each document. fields Can also define which fields to exclude by prefixing field names with <code>-</code></p>
</div><input type="text" class="" title="" placeholder="fields" disabled="" value=""></td></tr></tbody></table></div></div></div><div class="execute-wrapper"></div><div class="responses-wrapper"><div class="opblock-section-header"><h4>Responses</h4><label for="get_report_manager_settings_responses_select"><span>Response content type</span><div class="content-type-wrapper execute-content-type"><select aria-controls="get_report_manager_settings_responses" aria-label="Response content type" class="content-type" id="get_report_manager_settings_responses_select"><option value="application/json">application/json</option><option value="text/plain">text/plain</option><option value="application/zip">application/zip</option></select></div></label></div><div class="responses-inner"><table aria-live="polite" class="responses-table" id="get_report_manager_settings_responses" role="region"><thead><tr class="responses-header"><td class="col_header response-col_status">Code</td><td class="col_header response-col_description">Description</td></tr></thead><tbody><tr class="response " data-code="200"><td class="response-col_status">200</td><td class="response-col_description"><div class="response-col_description__inner"><div class="markdown"><p>Settings object.</p>
</div></div><div class="model-example"><ul class="tab" role="tablist"><li class="tabitem active" role="presentation"><button aria-controls="6fQBz6A=" aria-selected="true" class="tablinks" data-name="example" id="jRj7yBg=" role="tab">Example Value</button></li><li class="tabitem" role="presentation"><button aria-controls="gK70XaM=" aria-selected="false" class="tablinks" data-name="model" id="GMY2Vf0=" role="tab">Model</button></li></ul><div aria-hidden="false" aria-labelledby="jRj7yBg=" data-name="examplePanel" id="6fQBz6A=" role="tabpanel" tabindex="0"><div><div class="highlight-code"><pre class="example microlight" style="display: block; overflow-x: auto; padding: 0.5em; background: rgb(51, 51, 51); color: white;"><code class="language-json" style="white-space: pre;"><span>{

</div></div></div><div class="opblock-section"><div class="opblock-section-header"><div class="tab-header"><h4 class="opblock-title">Parameters</h4></div><div class="try-out"><button class="btn try-out__btn">Try it out </button></div></div><div class="parameters-container"><div class="table-container"><table class="parameters"><thead><tr><th class="col_header parameters-col_name">Name</th><th class="col_header parameters-col_description">Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr data-param-name="authorization" data-param-in="header"><td class="parameters-col_name"><div class="parameter__name">authorization</div><div class="parameter__type">string</div><div class="parameter__deprecated"></div><div class="parameter__in">(header)</div></td><td class="parameters-col_description"><div class="markdown"><p>The user's API token preceded by the keyword <code>Bearer</code> (with space between it and the token). </div><input type="text" class="" title="" placeholder="authorization" disabled="" value=""></td></tr><tr data-param-name="x-tenant-id" data-param-in="header"><td class="parameters-col_name"><div class="parameter__name">x-tenant-id</div><div class="parameter__type">string</div><div class="parameter__deprecated"></div><div class="parameter__in">(header)</div></td><td class="parameters-col_description"><div class="markdown"><p>Used to define the Tenant ID on behalf of whom the operation will be executed</p>
</div><input type="text" class="" title="" placeholder="x-tenant-id" disabled="" value=""></td></tr><tr data-param-name="body" data-param-in="body"><td class="parameters-col_name"><div class="parameter__name required">body<span>&nbsp;*</span></div><div class="parameter__type">object</div><div class="parameter__deprecated"></div><div class="parameter__in">(body)</div></td><td class="parameters-col_description"><div class="markdown"><p>Settings to update</p>
</div><div class="model-example"><ul class="tab" role="tablist"><li class="tabitem active" role="presentation"><button aria-controls="s12nftE=" aria-selected="true" class="tablinks" data-name="example" id="GTBzJo4=" role="tab">Example Value</button></li><li class="tabitem" role="presentation"><button aria-controls="YcyCOZQ=" aria-selected="false" class="tablinks" data-name="model" id="K4Sn2Yc=" role="tab">Model</button></li></ul><div aria-hidden="false" aria-labelledby="GTBzJo4=" data-name="examplePanel" id="s12nftE=" role="tabpanel" tabindex="0"><div class="body-param" data-param-name="body" data-param-in="body"><div class="highlight-code"><pre class="body-param__example microlight" style="display: block; overflow-x: auto; padding: 0.5em; background: rgb(51, 51, 51); color: white;"><code class="language-json" style="white-space: pre;"><span>{
</span><span>  </span><span class="hljs-attr">"holdReports"</span><span>: </span><span style="color: rgb(252, 194, 140);">false</span><span>,
</span><span>  </span><span class="hljs-attr">"features"</span><span>: {
</span><span>    </span><span class="hljs-attr">"archiving"</span><span>: </span><span style="color: rgb(252, 194, 140);">true</span><span>,
</span><span>    </span><span class="hljs-attr">"priority"</span><span>: </span><span style="color: rgb(252, 194, 140);">true</span><span>,
</span><span>    </span><span class="hljs-attr">"eventBased"</span><span>: </span><span style="color: rgb(252, 194, 140);">true</span><span>,
</span><span>    </span><span class="hljs-attr">"csv_export"</span><span>: </span><span style="color: rgb(252, 194, 140);">true</span><span>,
</span><span>    </span><span class="hljs-attr">"xls_export"</span><span>: </span><span style="color: rgb(252, 194, 140);">true</span><span>,
</span><span>    </span><span class="hljs-attr">"xls_combined_export"</span><span>: </span><span style="color: rgb(252, 194, 140);">true</span><span>,
</span><span>    </span><span class="hljs-attr">"pdf_export"</span><span>: </span><span style="color: rgb(252, 194, 140);">true</span><span>,
</span><span>    </span><span class="hljs-attr">"url_export"</span><span>: </span><span style="color: rgb(252, 194, 140);">true</span><span>,
</span><span>    </span><span class="hljs-attr">"jasper_pdf_export"</span><span>: </span><span style="color: rgb(252, 194, 140);">true</span><span>,
</span><span>    </span><span class="hljs-attr">"jasper_xls_export"</span><span>: </span><span style="color: rgb(252, 194, 140);">true</span><span>,
</span><span>    </span><span class="hljs-attr">"sftp_enabled"</span><span>: </span><span style="color: rgb(252, 194, 140);">true</span><span>,
</span><span>    </span><span class="hljs-attr">"use_report_owner_for_non_sisense_user"</span><span>: </span><span style="color: rgb(252, 194, 140);">true</span><span>,
</span><span>    </span><span class="hljs-attr">"send_reports_to_non_sisense_users"</span><span>: </span><span style="color: rgb(252, 194, 140);">true</span><span>,
</span><span>    </span><span class="hljs-attr">"show_error_on_sending_to_non_sisense_users"</span><span>: </span><span style="color: rgb(252, 194, 140);">true</span><span>
</span>  },
<span>  </span><span class="hljs-attr">"options"</span><span>: {
</span><span>    </span><span class="hljs-attr">"non_sisense_user_email"</span><span>: </span><span style="color: rgb(162, 252, 162);">"string"</span><span>,
</span><span>    </span><span class="hljs-attr">"delete_exported_files"</span><span>: </span><span style="color: rgb(252, 194, 140);">true</span><span>,
</span><span>    </span><span class="hljs-attr">"local_export_path"</span><span>: </span><span style="color: rgb(162, 252, 162);">"string"</span><span>,
</span><span>    </span><span class="hljs-attr">"allow_empty_excel"</span><span>: </span><span style="color: rgb(252, 194, 140);">true</span><span>,
</span><span>    </span><span class="hljs-attr">"sftp_servers"</span><span>: </span><span style="color: rgb(162, 252, 162);">"[{ \"host\": \"10.50.55.91\", \"port\": \"8899\", \"username\": \"username\", \"password\": \"sftpserverpassword\", \"serverName\": \"My Server\", \"allowOnlyToGroups\": [\"609048af5da057002cbc4e43\"] }]"</span><span>,
</span><span>    </span><span class="hljs-attr">"attachment_size_limit"</span><span>: </span><span style="color: rgb(211, 99, 99);">25</span><span>,
</span><span>    </span><span class="hljs-attr">"time_zone"</span><span>: </span><span style="color: rgb(162, 252, 162);">"America/Kentucky/Louisville"</span><span>,
</span><span>    </span><span class="hljs-attr">"email_template"</span><span>: </span><span style="color: rgb(162, 252, 162);">"640cf81A46C9205E12d1EeBa"</span><span>,
</span><span>    </span><span class="hljs-attr">"default_error_email"</span><span>: [
</span><span>      </span><span style="color: rgb(162, 252, 162);">""</span><span>
</span>    ],
<span>    </span><span class="hljs-attr">"csv_export_date_format"</span><span>: {
</span><span>      </span><span class="hljs-attr">"full_date"</span><span>: </span><span style="color: rgb(162, 252, 162);">"EEEE, MMMM d, y"</span><span>,
</span><span>      </span><span class="hljs-attr">"long_date"</span><span>: </span><span style="color: rgb(162, 252, 162);">"MMMM d, y"</span><span>,
</span><span>      </span><span class="hljs-attr">"medium_date"</span><span>: </span><span style="color: rgb(162, 252, 162);">"MMM d, y"</span><span>,
</span><span>      </span><span class="hljs-attr">"short_date"</span><span>: </span><span style="color: rgb(162, 252, 162);">"M/d/yy"</span><span>
</span>    }
